- **codex-setup**: Get the Codex CLI ready so /bymax-quality:code-review can run its independent second review. - **command_specs**: The command system is the only place a user action gets an identity **and** a display. One `AppCommand` is presented, shortcut, dispatched, and exposed over IPC. That is how keyboard, menu, toolbar, command bar, tooltips, and RPC stay one verb.
